# a224eb8 Merge pull request #7 from steven91007/docs-bilingual

## Summary
Merges PR #7 into master: README.md becomes English with README.zh-TW.md as the full Traditional Chinese version (each links to the other), the jobseekers-mcp submodule moves to its bilingual README, and gitkb notes are added for 1b93461, the PR #6 merge and the README split. Relative to master this is cc6e82b plus its notes commit.

## Why
Lands the English-first, switchable documentation on master.

## Risks
Two language versions of the README must be kept in sync by hand.

## Files
- `README.md` (M, +90/-87): Rewritten in English: the four ways to use the project, installation, CLI with region presets and the visa check (quoting the Chinese UI labels with translations), Discord bot setup and commands, design notes, tests, gitkb, the MCP submodule and the AI job agent. [note](../changes/411353bce3c3a0c0bec93f1663d72548c37f0ac0029a51369e63bf35009a6124.md)
- `README.zh-TW.md` (A, +290/-0): New Traditional Chinese README: the previous Chinese content plus a translation of the AI job agent section, with a switch back to the English README. [note](../changes/42e703b56033bcb4b1775592115a81ce5d180c73fb99354c1039b38c515167db.md)
- `jobseekers-mcp` (M, +1/-1): Submodule moved to c4ca767 (English README.md plus README.zh-TW.md in jobseekers-mcp). [note](../changes/90d73b8ec96ceb6f7a20a3293ef21e1de9c9755dbb63f2b3fa1da206d406cc10.md)
